# QA test plan: lookout tower

Written before testing. Numbers from other agents are "claimed" until t-qa reproduces them in its own baton slot (or Editor-free, on files, where noted). **Targets below are frozen at rev 1.0.** A target changes after a result only if the target itself was wrong (arithmetic, units, a datum misread, or a conflict with a ruling), t-qa agrees before the change, and the report discloses it. Rows marked **OPEN (pre-result)** are readings t-qa has asked the owner or team-lead to settle before the first measurement; they close in a plan revision, not in a test.

## Ground rules

- **Baton:** `unity` commands, batchmode, tests and anything saved under Assets/, Packages/ or ProjectSettings/ only inside a t-producer grant; reply "started" at once; `unity status` first; release with what changed and what is verified. qa probe code is staged in the scratchpad (`tqa/`), offline-compiled, landed only in the slot and removed at release unless t-td wants it kept.
- **Known test side effect (rev 1.5):** the EditMode water rebuild (WaterBuildTests) rewrites `Assets/World/BellsBend/Water/WaterTile.asset` with line endings only, and batchmode runs can do the same to `ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset` (fish pod, confirmed by team-lead). Every slot checks `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol --stat` is empty for them and restores with `git checkout -- <path>` before release; neither is a tower change.
- **Snapshot binding:** every result names the tree it ran on: `git status --short` plus SHA-256 of World.unity, MapConfig.asset, the tower FBX, prefab, materials, atlas, parameter table, the terrain tile(s) under the tower, the road map and the build. Pre-tower list: `TestResults/tower-qa/pre-tower-baseline.sha256` (1,830 files under Assets/, Packages/, ProjectSettings/, tools/, taken 2026-10-11T03:55Z; 0 LFS pointers; only tower file present: tools/blender/lookout/ wrapper, outside Assets/).
- **No literals from results.** Tests read targets from the parameter table, `landmarks.json` / the terrain pipeline, MapConfig and the design spec at test time. t-qa greps every tower test for numbers equal to a measured result (heights, coordinates, tri counts, times) and for assertions that compare the model with itself.
- **Tower-absent twin.** Every still and every pixel or raycast measurement is paired with the same shot or probe with the measured thing absent (tower root disabled; water or barrier hidden for P3; handrail colliders off for C2's detector). Changed pixels are counted (|ΔL| ≥ 0.0015 linear Rec.709). A measurement that passes on the twin is void.
- **Flaky = fail.** Gating PlayMode suites (C1-C3, I1-I4) run 3x at pinned `Time.captureFramerate = 60`; all 3 must pass. The wildlife walk is graded under the P4 rule below.
- **Null wiring.** After any scene or prefab wiring, the owner greps the YAML for `fileID: 0` on the fields set; t-qa repeats the grep at verification.
- **Player windows:** no launch without team-lead's OK (t-qa -> t-producer -> team-lead). `Get-Process WashedAshorePOC` empty first; foreground; `-monitor 1`.
- **Web:** not built, not tested, by Noah's direction.
- **Evidence folders (TestResults/, gitignored):** `tower-qa/` (t-qa), `tower-art/` (t-artist), `tower-tools/` (t-tools), `tower-level/` (t-level), `tower-eng/` (t-engineer), `tower-look/` (director gate stills).

## Targets of record

Imperial values are from the spec and research.md / `digests/mc39-design-r1-1.md`; metres = feet x 0.3048, computed here once. Tests compute them from the parameter table, not from this table.

| Item | Target | Tolerance | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Base square (back to back of the leg angles at the bed plates, Y = 0) | **RULED by team-lead (rev 1.3, before results; a consequence of the height decision, disclosed, not an assist):** 20'0¼" = 6.1024 m for the 86'3" standard (digest line 24 marks the fraction "~"; t-artist states the value and its straight-taper derivation in the parameter table and qa grades against that stated value; qa's own taper check: 6.102-6.115 m). The spec's 19'0½" = 5.804 m is the 79'6" tower's | ±0.05 m | team-lead via t-producer |
| Top square at the cab floor | 7'0" = 2.134 m | ±0.05 m (qa: same as the base) | spec T1 |
| Leg taper | straight: every leg splice point within 0.02 m of the base-to-top line | qa | digest "straight-line taper" |
| Tower height | **86'3" = 26.289 m** footing top to cab floor (rev 1.2: t-artist's count, `tower-height-decision.md`: 8 panels on the photo's front leg, 18-20 treads in the bottom flight, projective fit RMS 14 px vs 77 px for 79'6"; decided before any build of record). Eaves 28.575 m. Height stays a parameter; 79'6" = 24.232 m still builds | ±0.10 m | spec fixed inputs, T1 |
| Height datum | **RULED by team-lead (rev 1.1, before results; a spec-wording correction, disclosed in the report, not an assist).** The spec says "to the eaves"; the drawing's height series is "to cab floor / top of tower steel" (digest line 19, High). | Track | one new road id painted by the roads pipeline, continuous from the pad to a road or track that is itself painted in the game's road map (joining the nearest OSM track passes only if that track is painted through the pipeline too; joining the nearest painted road passes, disclosed as the reading "nearest road or track that exists in game"); road-map diff shows only that id; every existing road pixel and channel 2 (ShoreDistance) unchanged. Length (450-900 m expected; research.md: nearest road about 900 m) is reported as a note: "short" is not gradeable at a fixed site (rev 1.1, fixed before anything is painted) | exact | spec P2 |
| View | (rev 1.1, t-level's definitions, accepted) eye = cab floor + 1.65 m at the standing position at each cab side; quadrant = ±45° of N/E/S/W; occluders = terrain plus real tree crowns (position, scaled height, crown radius); shoreline visible in a quadrant = **(rev 1.5, Noah's ruling, FROZEN)** ≥ 50 m of contiguous bank length with open water visible within N = 20 m outside the bank (N = MapConfig.shoreRampMeters), measured in-engine with real trees, ≥ 3 of 4 quadrants; the literal rev 1.1 form (≥ 50 m contiguous inner-bank waterline in clear sight) is measured and reported beside it; north barrier visible = ≥ 25 m contiguous top rail in clear sight; eye above every crown within 60 m; a side is blocked if > 25% of its azimuths have a crown above the horizontal eye line within 150 m, at most one side blocked (N10). Stills: water-hidden twin with ≥ 200 changed px below the horizon at 1920x1080 in ≥ 3 of 4; barrier-hidden twin on the north still with changed px ≥ the projected length of 25 m of rail (about 9 px). All at the shipped far clip and technique. team-lead: the barrier (2,218 m) and the 1 km view are spec targets and are not reworded to fit the 1000 m far clip | exact | spec P3 |
| Climb time | 60.0-120.0 s, first flight's foot to feet on the cab floor, W only, hatch opening included; and within ±10% of the design formula (Σ lane length / stairAscendSpeed + Σ landing crossing / towerWalkSpeed) with built lengths; the same within 2% at 30 and 240 FPS | exact | spec C1, N9, design §2.4, §9 |
| Stress | 0 escapes, 0 stuck states, every flight, both directions, 3 seeds | exact | spec C2 |
| Interact | E and gamepad South via the Input System; target at 2.49 m, none at 2.51 m (`interactRange` 2.5, never above); one prompt line, `{key} – {Verb} {noun}`, exact strings of design §3.2; South interacts without jumping when a prompt shows | exact | spec I1, N12, design §3 |
| Hatch | hinged, 1.0 s ≤ duration ≤ 2.5 s (`hatchSeconds` 1.4), monotonic, peak ≤ 150°/s, ends at 0° and `hatchOpenAngle` ±0.5°; never moves without input; no upward player speed > 2 m/s; under-floor duck: eye height a function of lane position only (±0.005 m), never under 1.0 m, eye ≥ 0.20 m from floor and leaf | exact | spec I2, N13, design §4 |
| Firefinder | fixed engraved numerals every 10° (000 at +Z, 090 at +X, each within ±0.5°) plus a pointer on the alidade: qa's reading of "a world-space number on the brass ring" (no changing digits, no canvas). After a 90° turn: BearingDegrees changed 90 ±0.5, pointer azimuth (±0.5°) and the sight's flattened view direction equal it within ±1°; `Ring_Numeral_000..350` empties and the engraved mesh agree. FOV `firefinderFov` 45 ±0.5 in use (inside qa's 35-60 bound), 70 after. E tap (< `firefinderTapSeconds`) leaves, E hold rotates, Esc leaves | ±1° | spec I3, N14, design §5 |
| Panes | 72 panes (4 sides x 2 sashes x 9); 18-26 missing (25-36%), not the same count on all four sides (N6) | exact | spec fixed inputs |

**Design targets (rev 1.2):** `tower-design-spec.md` r1.1 §9 (T-C1a to T-I4c) is adopted as the C and I targets of record, read by tests from `LookoutTuning` and the built lane data. Where it is stricter than this plan it replaces it: eye ≥ 0.20 m from every collider and visual mesh (C3); stuck = < 0.2 m of progress in 2 s; feet step ≤ speed in force x dt + 0.02 m (r1.2: measured on the feet, because the approved hatch duck moves the eye; the eye keeps the teleport bound and T-I2e). Stuck = < 0.1 m of progress in 5 s (qa's definition, adopted in r1.2; the shut-hatch gate on the top flight is the one named exception). Range: PlayMode 2.4 / 2.6 m and EditMode 2.49 / 2.51 m. Jump is ignored on the whole tower: t-director, `ruling/lookout-tower-o1-o2` (studio:decisions), verbatim: "Information items, no objection: jump ignored on the tower; sprint has no effect on stairs; bearing read from fixed engraved numerals with no dynamic text (matches N14)." C2 still presses Space. The 0.25 m tower capsule is a technique detail for t-td's ruling. The hatch duck (O1) and the 4 s "E – Leave firefinder" line (O2) are approved in the same ruling and graded by T-I2e and T-I1b/T-I3c (absent at 5 s, back on a fresh use). The revision after t-td's technique ruling is diffed by qa: no §9 target may move without qa's agreement. A retune = the bold `LookoutTuning` fields inside their ranges, after Noah's I5 rating.

### Pre-result findings sent to the owners (rev 1.0)

1. **Height datum** (above): spec vs drawing. Ruled by team-lead in rev 1.1.
2. **Top flight pitch** (above). Closed in rev 1.2.
3. **Player capsule vs stair width:** CharacterController radius 0.4 m (0.8 m wide) against a 0.61 m stair with handrails on both sides. The collision proxy cannot equal the visual rails. Technique is t-td's gate item; T1 measures the visual mesh, C2 grades the proxy.
4. **Camera far clip 1000 m** (World.unity:83576): a tower "in view from 1 km" sits on the far plane, and the north barrier is 2,218 m from the summit, so the cab cannot draw it (P3, G4, G5, N10, T4). Needs a t-td decision before P3 or T4 is measured. team-lead (rev 1.1): the targets stand; t-td picks a technique with measured Windows FPS and water/fish regression cost, which P4 and T4 then cover.
5. **Gamepad South is already Jump** (PlayerController.cs:76), and I1 puts Interact on South.
6. **I3 key conflict:** the spec has E both rotating the bearing and exiting. t-designer resolves it in the design spec; qa grades that resolution, provided Esc exits and Q/E or the mouse rotates. Resolved in design r1.1: tap leaves, hold rotates.
7. **Base square** (rev 1.2, above): the spec's 19'0½" is the 79'6" tower's; the decided 86'3" tower's is ~20'0¼". Ruled by team-lead in rev 1.3.

(6) Rebuilds for unrelated reasons (shutters, look fixes) must not change collider or lane data; qa's diff proves it; if lane data changes, the lane-end checks rerun and (5) applies. **qa's evidence rule for "the check is wrong", all four per red, kept by team-lead:** (a) the quantity is recomputed from a source the test does not use (qa's probe on the built colliders, the manifest figure, or arithmetic from the parameter table) and disagrees with the test's value; (b) the faulty line or assumption is identified and the fix changes how the value is computed or read, never the bar, the tolerance, the scope or the data source's authority; (c) the corrected check is shown able to fail by a negative control (its datum moved past the tolerance: 5 mm, 1 degree or a named structural change; t-engineer's staged LookoutBuiltTowerTests.Negative.cs, 19 cases, read by qa first); (d) the original failing output is kept beside the corrected run. Doubt goes to the build. A wrong bar is a target change by the usual route, not a shake-down fix. **Kept from t-td:** the ruling's text is frozen and circulated "final text, not issued" once qa has checked the table, and nothing in it changes to suit a test result; a check sorted wrong twice comes to qa and t-td before a third attempt; the setup throwing at import, the band[] and accept_at_bend comparisons and the lever check are named lane-end checks. **Superseded by rule (4):** qa's "issue only when green" and any second shake-down. **Listing rows before issue:** qa lists in known_findings only already-agreed findings restated from this export (QA-KF-2, 3, 4; landing 6 as the named under-target end); any other red table row is a build red for the fix round. **Lane data of record and build of record (agreed with t-td, t-producer):** lane data of record is fixed by the build imported for the shake-down and named in the ruling by manifest hash and `lane_data_digest`; the build of record for T3 and T4 is the final visual export, which must carry the same lane data. The digest (SHA-256 over a canonical form, numbers to six decimals) covers: schema, frame, levels and deck thickness, reach_bisection, area bounds, flights whole, exit lanes, landings, hatch, the cab's interior square, floor and walk rectangle, every COL_/CLIP_ proxy (name, kind, pivot, sorted vertices), every LANE_/HATCH_ empty (name, position), and the check values that shape lanes, named one by one; out: visuals, counts, rails, pads, corridor eye rows, anchors, check results, the three known_findings fields, the bars eye_clear and run_room_min, and free-text keys by name. A second `interaction_data_digest` covers hatch, sash and firefinder hinge data. qa's conditions: the build stops on a check value or string key in no list; two wrapper-run negative controls (a 0.01 mm proxy or empty move changes the lane digest only; a hinge-axis change changes the interaction digest only); any shutter or stay proxies are in the first imported build; and the digest is not the only evidence: qa compares colliders and LANE_/HATCH_ empties on the imported prefab at 0.1 mm between imports. Also recorded from t-artist's build: the drawing's 5'2" on T-1004 is read by t-artist as the width of the two-flight strip (built 1.42 m against 1.575 m, -0.155 m, outside ±0.10 m): held for the layout table, not tweaked in isolation; pane counting is done by qa from the mesh (glass on its own material slot, 3x3 cells per sash), not from the manifest (manifest: 22 missing of 72).

Predictions from t-artist's railed dev manifest (t-engineer, nothing run), all fed into team-lead's stair-layout root-cause check: capsule-fit margins of 5 mm (flight 7's foot) and 1 mm (flight 8's foot); T-C2c part (1) would fail at flight 8 (diagN7_2 0.162 m from the eye path) and flight 7 (diagW6_1 0.196 m); diagW6_1 3.4 cm and midW6_1 0.9 cm inside the body corridor. Twins, both in the suite and failing it if silent: landings/cab with CLIP_ walls off; flights with walls off and slack widened to 1 m in a copy, joined 0.15 m off centre, lane-lateral against the authored slack must fire on all 8 (no test-only A/D path in the controller). The walls-on C2 run also reports the maximum lateral offset per flight after the grace (≤ authored slack + 0.01 m). T-C1e: totals compared across captureDeltaTime 1/30, 1/120, 1/240 within 2% after one pre-registered correction for the script's per-frame condition waits (total minus N x dt / 2, N logged; raw and corrected both reported; raw inside 60.0-120.0 s at each rate). From here a detector change needs qa's agreement first, and after its first walls-on run also needs the definition itself to have been wrong.

T1's "flights running diagonally between interior corners with triangular landings" gets a per-flight check: for each of the 8 flights qa's probe reads from the built FBX the foot and head in plan, the heading relative to the faces, the corner of each landing, riser and tread counts, and each landing's plan shape and deck dimensions, and compares them with a drawing-derived table for the 86'3" tower (t-artist, sheet cited per number; checked independently by qa against the research images, reporting which numbers qa could read, could not read, or reads differently). A difference passes only where re-runnable arithmetic shows the drawing cannot close at 86'3" and t-director approves the built answer by name (disclosed interpretation); otherwise it is a T1 fail. ### Disclosure: P3 shoreline definition questioned after an offline result (rev 1.3, OPEN with team-lead)

- Definition fixed at rev 1.1 (04:03Z): ≥ 50 m contiguous inner-bank waterline in clear sight, ≥ 3 of 4 quadrants.
- t-level then ran an offline viewshed (at team-lead's request, before qa's reply; friction row 2): waterline visible in 1 of 4 quadrants, with a worst-case canopy and with terrain only.
- qa's independent terrain-only run (`tqa/viewshed_qa.py`, eye at ground + 26.289 + 1.65, no trees): waterline N/E/S/W 514 / 0 / 0 / 0 m (1 of 4); open water within 10 m of the bank 529 / 382 / 0 / 0 (2 of 4); within 30 m 611 / 1237 / 580 / 143 (4 of 4). The literal bar is unmeetable at the fixed site at any MC-39 height (the bank hides its own waterline), and the proposed reading passes or fails on N.
- qa's position to team-lead: (A) keep the literal definition and record the shoreline clause as a FAIL-finding, or (B) "open water visible within N m outside ≥ 50 m of contiguous bank", in-engine with real trees, with N = MapConfig.shoreRampMeters (20 m), a pipeline value fixed before the pod and not computed by qa before proposing it. qa recommends B and refuses N = 30 m as result-picked unless team-lead rules it. The literal result is reported beside whatever is ruled, under "narrowed after results".
- **RULED (rev 1.5), Noah via team-lead, 2026-10-11, verbatim: "Open water counts (Recommended)".** That is option B with N = 20 m. N, the 50 m length and the method are frozen whatever the result; a fail at N = 20 is a recorded FAIL finding. Reported under "narrowed after results", attributed to Noah's decision, with the literal waterline result (1 of 4 offline) beside it. North-barrier and above-canopy clauses unchanged. **ASSIST 1** (friction row 5).
- Offline prediction only, run by qa after the ruling (`TestResults/tower-qa/p3-offline-prediction.txt`; terrain only, no trees, eye at the cab centre): open water within 20 m, longest contiguous N/E/S/W = 570 / 764 / 219 / 31 m, i.e. 3 of 4 (west fails). Trees can only lower these. The in-engine probe from the standing eye with real trees is the record.
- Until ruled, the rev 1.1 definition stood. Rev 1.4: team-lead is putting it to Noah; nobody computes the N = 20 m result before his answer; his ruling is logged as ASSIST 1 (a reinterpretation proposed after a result).
- **Far plane (t-td early ruling, tower-td-note.md §4.7-4.8):** proposed 1200 m; nothing is drawn beyond about 1.15 km. T4 is graded at 1 km; "visible across the map" in T4's rationale is recorded as a stated limit, not claimed. This does not cover P3: the north barrier is 2,218 m from the cab and is graded on the north still at the shipped camera with a barrier-hidden twin, as are water pixels in the compass stills. Without a technique for it that clause is a recorded fail (told to t-td and t-producer before any result).

## Automatic-FAIL checks (run at every release that touches them, and again before the verdict)

| # | Check | How t-qa verifies | Owner if tripped |
|---|---|---|---|
| AF1 | Hand-made Blender or Unity steps not in a script | (a) Inventory: every tower file under Assets/ (FBX, textures, materials, prefab, .meta import settings, landmark, terrain pad, tree clearing, road paint, NavMesh, scene objects) is listed against the script and entry point that writes it. A file with no generator = FAIL. (b) Regeneration, in a qa slot: back up, delete the generated tower artefacts, run the scripts from the documented entry points only, and compare: FBX and textures byte-identical (T3 rule), .meta files identical, prefab and scene tower objects identical after YAML normalisation (fileIDs), terrain tile heights identical, tree and road data identical. Any difference that needs a manual step to remove = FAIL. (c) Blender is only ever run headless (`blender -b`); no .blend with hand edits in the repo; the build script opens no existing .blend | t-artist, t-tools, t-level, t-engineer |
| AF2 | Shipping or tracing the reference photos | SHA-256 of all 27 research images against every file under Assets/ and the Windows build's data folders: 0 matches. Perceptual check: dHash of every tower texture and atlas region against every research image at 3 scales: no near match (Hamming ≤ 10 of 64 is investigated by eye). Every tower texture is either an ambientCG CC0 file (asset id and licence text next to it, byte-identical to the download) or regenerated byte-identically by a script. No script reads the research `images/` folder (grep for the path, for image loads, for projection or bake-from-image calls) | t-artist |
| AF3 | Tower off-site or floating | P1 | t-level |
| AF4 | Can't climb to the cab, or escaping through the stairs | C1, C2 | t-engineer |
| AF5 | HUD beyond the single prompt line | UI audit in Play mode, before the climb, on the stairs, in the cab, at the prompt and inside the firefinder: all enabled Canvas / UIDocument / OnGUI / screen-space text and image elements, diffed against the same audit on the pre-tower scene: exactly one added text element, visible only while aiming at an interactable. Stills twin-diffed (prompt aimed vs not aimed): changed pixels confined to the prompt rectangle. No crosshair change, compass, bearing readout, banner, marker, scope mask, vignette or FOV pulse (N9, N11, N14) | t-engineer |
| AF6 | Breaking the web build | **Not tested, by Noah's direction.** Code review only (reported, not graded): no compute or geometry shaders, no APIs unavailable on WebGL2 in tower code | - |
| AF7 | Same blocker 3 times in a row, or > 3 assists | `tower-friction-log.md`, kept by t-qa from every agent's rows; count sent to t-producer on each change. Noah's planned T2 and I5 checks and team-lead's planned launches are evidence, not assists (water and fish precedent) | t-producer |
| AF8 | A greenlight note broken (qa adds; graded as a broken greenlight) | N1-N15 checked where measurable: no in-world text naming Burns, Bell, Montgomery Bell, Dickson or NHLR (grep strings, textures by eye); no grid dish, one bent mast and one bent Yagi; no HQ, shed, car, power line or cabin; no emissive material (emission keyword off, colour black); no outline, glow or icon on interactables; frame plumb (leg lines symmetric to 0.02 m) with no missing member against the parameter table; nothing auto-opens or closes. t-director's G1-G6 approvals are recorded verbatim with the file hashes approved | t-director rules |

## Pass criteria

| # | qa's independent check | Evidence | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| T1 | qa's own EditMode probe on the imported prefab (not the pod's test): reads mesh vertices of named parts (legs, footings, cab floor, wall, sash band, eaves, roof apex, each flight's stringers and nosings, each landing) and computes every row of the targets table; import is +Z forward, Y up, 1 unit = 1 m (root scale 1, rotation identity, bounds height equals the measured height, no 100x or 0.01x). Cross-check outside Unity: the same dimensions from the FBX in headless Blender. The pod's own test is reviewed for literals. Canary: the probe run on a build at the other standard height must report that height | `tower-qa/t1-measure.json`, `t1-blender.json`, pod XML | t-artist |
| T2 | Renders at the camera angles of `burns-nhlr-lutes-main.jpg` and `-feb2023.jpg`, side by side with the photos (the pairs live under TestResults/ and _bmad-output/ only). qa checks before the ask: same angle (the tower's projected corner and cab points within 3% of frame height of the photo's), tower-absent twin shows the tower is what changed, no photo pixels in the render half. Then Noah: "that's the tower: yes". Only after G1-G6 are approved and the automated rows pass | `tower-look/t2-*.png`, Noah's words | t-artist; t-qa asks |
| T3 | qa runs `build_mc39.ps1` twice itself, from a clean output folder each time: FBX, atlas and every exported file SHA-256 identical, after masking only the FBX creation time (rev 1.1, rule agreed with t-tools before any result: the seven int32 fields under FBXHeaderExtension/CreationTimeStamp, 28 bytes per FBX, found by node walk, accepted only if they decode inside the run's own time window at equal offsets in both files; nothing else masked in any file; `verify_repro.ps1` -> `TestResults/tower-repro/repro.json`; no widening without qa's agreement first). PYTHONHASHSEED is set by the wrapper itself. Parameter table is the only input: the script reads no other data file, no environment, no clock, no unseeded random (grep + a third run from a different working directory); changing height, pitch or decay in the table changes the output, and reverting restores the original hash. The FBX in Assets/ equals the script's output | `tower-qa/t3-run{1,2,3}.sha256`, `t3-diff.txt` | t-tools, t-artist |
| T4 | Mesh stats read by qa from the imported asset (triangles per LOD, material count across the prefab, texture dimensions, LODGroup transitions, impostor present). FPS: Windows player, existing `BellsBendFpsWalk` tooling extended by the pod with two tower poses (1 km with the tower centred, and standing in the cab), each run tower-on and tower-off; graded with the bars above; tower-in-view proven by a twin still at the 1 km pose: ≥ 20 changed pixels inside the tower's projected bounds and its projected height ≥ 15 px (expected about 20 px at 70° FOV), read as a pale lattice with a dark cab by t-director (G4). One session per Player.log. Web FPS: not tested | `tower-qa/t4-stats.json`, `tower-qa/t4/win-*.json`, twin stills | t-artist |
| P1 | qa `eval` in its slot: tower root XZ against the pipeline's projection of the `LookoutTower` landmark (recomputed, and checked against qa's own projection above); landmark present in `landmarks.json` and `map_vectors.json`; per footing: base plate, pad top, pad underside and `TerrainQuery.TryGroundHeight` at the pad's 4 corners. Terrain: heightmap of every tile diffed against the pre-tower baseline (hashes for untouched tiles, per-sample for the tower tile). The pad comes from the level-build script (AF1 regeneration) | `tower-qa/p1-site.json`, `p1-terrain-diff.json` | t-level |
| P2 | Tree instances and road map diffed against the baseline (counts, positions, the clearing ring, the corridor); the track is written by the roads pipeline (AF1); stills from the track and the base with tower-absent twins; scrub in the ring stays below the first landing (N8). t-director's G3 | `tower-qa/p2-trees.json`, `p2-roadmap-diff.png`, stills | t-level |
| P3 | qa's own raycast probe from the eye of a player standing at each cab side (controller eye height, not a free camera): rays to shoreline points (bank stations) and to the north barrier, blocked by terrain and tree colliders; per sector the count of visible shoreline points; twin: the same probe from ground level at the base must see fewer (proves the climb earns it). Stills: 4 compass shots from the cab with a water-hidden twin (water pixels counted per shot) and a barrier-hidden twin for the north shot; all at the game camera's own far clip. Tree-crown heights within 60 m against the cab floor | `tower-qa/p3-rays.json`, `p3-*.png` | t-level |
| P4 | Full EditMode and PlayMode XML on the final tree against qa's pre-tower baseline (below), same commands, same order. NavMesh: no walkable NavMesh inside any leg or pad footprint, a path exists round the tower, and a PlayMode run with animals routed across the site shows 0 agent positions inside a leg collider. Birds and robins: 0 flock or robin positions inside the tower's bounds in the bird runs and in a targeted run with a flock path through the tower; no perch or target point on the tower unless t-designer specifies one. L1-L4: terrain, tile and MapConfig hashes unchanged outside the P1 pad, so they stand; the tower tile re-runs them | `tower-qa/baseline/*`, `tower-qa/final/*`, `p4-compare.txt` | t-engineer, t-level; qa |
| C1 | Pod PlayMode test reviewed, then qa's own run: the real PlayerController driven through the Input System (W held, mouse yaw for the turns, E for the hatch), no transform writes after the start pose, no jump input; passes every flight; reaches the cab floor inside the time band; then a full descent to the ground. Mid-climb: stop 2 s on a flight (drift ≤ 0.05 m), turn round, descend one flight, climb again. Per frame: XZ step ≤ 1.5 x the zone's speed cap x dt and 3D step ≤ 1.5 x sprint x dt (no teleport; rev 1.1, t-engineer: on a 52° flight the 3D step is 1.62 x the horizontal one), both read from the tuning asset and the controller; input never disabled, no fade or camera cut (N9). Technique documented in the TD note and matches t-td's gate approval | `tower-qa/c1-climb.json`, XML x3 | t-engineer |
| C2 | Per flight and landing, up and down, 3 seeds: strafe into each rail, jump input at each third of the flight, sprint input held the length, and all three combined; plus a sprint run-up at real sprint speed from outside the climb zone into the first flight and off the top landings (rev 1.1, t-engineer). Escape = the capsule leaves the stair, landing and cab walk volumes, or is inside a frame member's volume, or is > 0.5 m below its supporting surface. Stuck = held input with < 0.1 m progress in 5 s where the path is open. Launch = upward speed above the jump's take-off speed sqrt(2 x JumpHeight x -Gravity) + 10% (read from PlayerController; rev 1.1 corrects "apex speed"), or any frame displacement > 1.5 x sprint x dt. If the design spec disables jump in the climb zone (with t-director's yes under N9), the test still presses Space and asserts no escape, stuck or launch. Detector twin: with the rail colliders disabled the same run must report escapes | `tower-qa/c2-stress.json`, XML x3 | t-engineer |
| C3 | Perimeter walk both ways round the pedestal; stand at all four sides, at both sashes; at each stand, yaw 360° and pitch ±80°: no wall, roof, sash, pedestal or hatch geometry within the near-plane corner distance of the camera (0.175 m at near 0.1, 70°, 16:9), measured against the render meshes, plus stills checked for see-through. Eye height clears every sill (N10): eye 1.65 m against the 1.067 m lower wall, measured | `tower-qa/c3-cab.json`, stills | t-engineer |
| I1 | EditMode: the interactable interface and interactor have no tower-specific types (reusable); Interact is an Input System action bound to `<Keyboard>/e` and `<Gamepad>/buttonSouth`. PlayMode: prompt shown at 2.4 m and not at 2.6 m, hidden when not aiming or when occluded; E and a virtual gamepad South press both fire; the prompt is the only UI (AF5). No outline, glow, pulse or icon (N12) | XML x3, `tower-qa/i1-*.png` | t-engineer |
| I2 | Sequence: open, climb in, close, stand on it 10 s (drift ≤ 0.02 m, grounded every frame), open while clear, climb down. Animation: hinge angle continuous and monotonic per move, duration ≥ 1.0 s, no state change without input over 30 s. Abuse: open it while standing on it, close it while in the opening, jump under it closed: no escape, stuck or launch (C2 definitions). A refused press (rev 1.1) = hinge angle unchanged and logged as refused; it passes provided the player always has a way through (step off the leaf and it opens; step out of the opening and it closes), both tested | `tower-qa/i2-hatch.json`, XML x3 | t-engineer |
| I3 | Enter: player position fixed (≤ 0.01 m), move input ignored, FOV at the design value inside 35-60°, no mask or overlay (AF5). Q/E and mouse each rotate; after a 90° turn the number on the ring equals the sight's true world bearing (0 = north = +Z, clockwise) ±1°, read from the world-space object's own text or digits, which is a world-space renderer on the brass ring and not a canvas; no live map or marker (N14). Esc exits, and E exits or rotates per the design spec; FOV, control and prompt restored | `tower-qa/i3-firefinder.json`, stills, XML x3 | t-engineer |
| I4 | One hinged sash per side opens and closes on E (4 of 8); fixed sashes show no prompt. Each sash's missing-pane set is identical open and closed (the same pane objects, rigidly moved: pane ids and local vertex hash equal). Pane count and spread per the targets table. No auto-close; the open sash doesn't clip the player or the camera | `tower-qa/i4-sashes.json`, XML x3 | t-engineer, t-artist |
| I5 | After G3-G6 and qa's automated pass, on a Windows build: one full climb by Noah; climb, view, interactions each **right / not right**; all three "right". A retune = a change made in answer to his rating (max 2); qa re-runs the affected rows before the re-ask. Launch arguments and a 3-line script go t-qa -> t-producer -> team-lead. His words are recorded verbatim | ratings in the report | t-designer (retunes) |

## P4 baseline and grading

- **Recorded results (fish-qa-report.md, final fish tree):** PlayMode full suite 98 cases: 81 pass, 13 fail, 4 skipped (`TestResults/fish-qa/ab/full1.xml`). Fails: FishCensusTests (F3) 1, FishBehaviourTests (F6 seed 303) 1, FishSightingTests (F7) 9, BirdFrameTime 1, WildlifeFrameTime 1 (BB-QA-7). Skips: 2 InputSystem package tests, 2 explicit fish runs. EditMode 200 cases, 199 pass, with W1_NoLiteralWaterHeight then fixed in #26 (test-only). Wildlife sightingRate seeds 101/202/303: filtered 112-114 / 180 / 181-182; full suite 112 / 180 / 185 (W-QA-1). Birds flushSeen 42 / 60 / 78 in every run.
- **qa's pre-tower baseline (first baton slot, untouched branch):** EditMode full x1; PlayMode full x1 with the fish pod's command of record (`unity test --mode PlayMode --filter '^(?!WashedAshore\.Tests\.PlayMode\.Fish)'`, which ran all 98 cases in `fish-qa/ab/full1.xml`; same process, same fixture order); then the fish pod's filtered set (`WashedAshore\.Tests\.(Wildlife|Birds)|WashedAshore\.Tests\.PlayMode\.(?!Fish)`) x1, and a second full run if the slot allows. One script for the baseline and every later comparison run (scratchpad `tqa/regress.sh`), compared per case with `tqa/xml_cases.py`. XML, logs, sightings JSONs and hashes in `TestResults/tower-qa/baseline/`.
- **Gate:** per test case, the final tree's result equals the baseline's (pass stays pass; the known fails may stay failed and are not tower regressions; a known fail turning into a pass is reported). New tower tests are listed separately. Seeded readings: birds exact; wildlife per gating seed within [baseline min - 1, max + 1] for the same run type (full against full, filtered against filtered), with the W-QA-1 full-suite reading (303 at 185) accepted as known. Fish F3/F6/F7 numbers are reported against the fish report's values.
- **Baseline result (slot 1, 04:00-04:31Z, `TestResults/tower-qa/baseline/summary.txt`).** EditMode 200/200. PlayMode full: 100 cases, 83 pass, 13 fail, 4 skipped; against the fish record 0 cases changed and the order is identical; the 13 fails are the known ones (F3 x1, F6 seed 303 x1, F7 x9, BirdFrameTime, WildlifeFrameTime); 2 cases added by the fish pod's last slot (F8Spawn_FacingHolds x2) pass. Filtered 68/68. Wildlife 112 / 180 / 182 (pctGe5 1 / 12 / 13) in both runs; birds 42 / 60 / 78. **Gates of record:** per case, final == baseline; birds exact; wildlife filtered 111-115 / 179-181 / 180-183 (pctGe5 0-2 / 11-13 / 12-14); wildlife full 111-113 / 179-181 / 181-186 with pctGe5 4-14 at seed 303 (known values 182 here and 185 in the fish pod's full run: W-QA-1). One full run only (second dropped, two holders queued). Tree hash-identical to the 03:55Z list at release.
- **A changed reading is a P4 finding.** The clearing and the NavMesh rebake change the world near the summit; if a seeded wildlife or bird reading moves outside its gate, that is recorded as a P4 fail unless team-lead rules otherwise before the verdict, with the evidence (which animals, how far from the tower) beside it.
- BirdFrameTime / WildlifeFrameTime fail before the tower (BB-QA-7) and are not graded.
- **Regenerated data (rev 1.1).** Hashed before and after every placement step: `Data/terrain/build/` (bank_stations.json, map_vectors.json, manifest.json, tiles), LevelRoads.png, the terrain tile assets, tree and detail instances, the LM_ landmark objects. Expected changes, accepted as data: map_vectors.json gains LookoutTower; LevelRoads.png gains one road id; TD_2_2 heights change inside the pad square and its trees leave the 19.3 m ring; (rev 1.7, confirmed to t-level before slot 3) the ground build rewrites the tile assets the clearing and track touch (the script's printed list is the record; estimate TD_2_2, TD_2_1, TD_1_1, TD_1_2), so those files' hashes may change while their content does not: proof is by content against qa's own pre-placement snapshot (`TestResults/tower-qa/baseline/terrain-content/`: per tile, SHA-256 of heights, tree instances, each detail layer and the splat, plus raw tree records), beside t-level's Snapshot/Compare. In every rewritten tile other than TD_2_2: heights, trees and details identical; splat identical until the rut paint, then different only along the track. LevelZones.png differs only inside R; LevelMaps.asset and ZoneConfig.asset gain only the named fields; World.unity gains objects only. A rewritten tile not on the printed list, or any tree or detail outside R that differs, is a finding. Must be identical: bank_stations.json (read by WaterTestKit, FishTestKit, FishSignInGameTests), LevelRoads.png channel 2 (ShoreDistance, read by fish), LM_McCordBluff / LM_BuzzardBluff / LM_OutdoorCenter. Anything else that differs is a finding.
- **NavMesh / avoidance clause: RULED by team-lead (rev 1.4): not applicable at this site, with proof; no rebake; not an assist.** t-level: the tower is 444 m east and 405 m north of the 512 m habitat window, which is the whole WildlifeNavMesh bake volume. Proof set, measured by qa in a slot: bake volume and habitat bounds against the tower position; NavMesh.SamplePosition within 50 m of every leg returns nothing; the closest approach of any animal, robin or flock over all seeds from the run dumps; WildlifeNavMesh.asset and the habitat assets hash-identical to the baseline. Reported as "not applicable, with proof", not as a pass; follow-up: "tower outside the wildlife window; avoidance untested". The spec says "rebaked", so this is disclosed as a criterion reading.
- Note for the report: the map's highest built point is 220 m NNW of the site (Y 92.73 vs 90.76, t-level); the location is a fixed input.

## Noah's checks (spec evidence, not assists)

Sent once each to t-producer for team-lead, after t-director's G3-G6 approvals and qa's automated pass.
- **T2:** two side-by-side PNG paths, one question: "Is that the tower: yes or no?"
- **I5:** the launch command, then three lines: (1) walk the track to the base and climb to the cab with W, turning on each landing; (2) open the hatch, step into the cab, close it, open a sash on each side and look out; (3) use the firefinder, turn it, leave it, and climb back down. Then: "Climb: right or not right? View: right or not right? Interactions: right or not right?"

| # | When | What Noah did | Why the pod couldn't |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2026-10-11 ~04:10Z | Ruled on the P3 "shoreline visible" reading ("Open water counts (Recommended)"), a reinterpretation proposed after an offline result | the literal waterline bar is unmeetable at the fixed site (bank hides its own waterline); a spec-wording decision only Noah can make |
| 2 | 2026-10-11 ~16:10Z | Relaxed P3 after offline results existed: "I dont' think we need to thin the fog. don't get to hung up on that instruction of having to see the water from 3 out of 4 directions." | the shoreline and north-barrier clauses could not be met from the cab without thinning fog and special drawing, changes to the look of the whole scene that only Noah can weigh against the criterion; the clauses are now recorded, not gating |

## Verdict rule

- **PASS:** T1-T4, P1-P4, C1-C3, I1-I5 pass (web items recorded as not tested, by Noah's direction), no AF tripped, N1-N15 hold, 0 assists.
- **PASS WITH ASSISTS (n):** as PASS with 1 ≤ n ≤ 3.
- **FAIL:** any AF, any row failing at the end of the pod, a third "not right" on I5, T2 not "yes", or a greenlight note broken.
